:robot: I have created a release *beep* *boop*
---


## [0.22.0](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/compare/v0.23.1...v0.22.0) (2026-11-08)


###   BREAKING CHANGES

* **env-schema:** perSystem.spindrift.agents.bashTimeoutMs is now nullOr int. A Consumer that quotes the value, as in bashTimeoutMs = "1800000", fails evaluation with a type error; drop the quotes. The DRIVER_BASH_TIMEOUT_MS env var and the --driver-bash-timeout-ms flag still carry a string, checked in the Box as before; the flag table only labels the flag int in --help and the man page. MIGRATING.md carries the upgrade note.
* **entrypoint:** exec box after the first Driver run
* ORCHESTRATOR_ENABLED, --orchestrator and dispatch.orchestrator.enable no longer exist. The direct driver-exec path they selected is gone.
* **entrypoint:** always hand the pass to the orchestrator
* **promptassembly:** fragments/review-loop-inline.md and its REVIEW_LOOP_INLINE_STEP var no longer exist.
* **orchestrator:** ORCHESTRATOR_ENABLED (dispatch.orchestrator.enable) now defaults to on. A Consumer whose custom reviewPrompt lacks the VERDICT: marker now fails at build time unless it sets orchestrator.enable = false.
* **doctor:** `spindrift doctor` exits 2 instead of 0 on a host whose podman machine has less RAM than MEMORY_LIMIT x MAX_PARALLEL needs. See MIGRATING.md for the remedies.
* **registryroutes:** a routes file declaring go-path, gradle-path or cargo-registries no longer parses. Move each into that route's [routes.ecosystems.<name>] block; the launch gate prints the equivalent stanza.
* **registry-routes:** retire upstream-base-url and enforce-allowlist
* **launcher:** the perSystem.spindrift.infra.registryProxyCredential* flake options and the --registry-proxy-* flags no longer exist. A flake still setting one fails eval; a script still passing one gets an unknown flag. Use registryProxyRoutesFile and a routes file instead; see MIGRATING.md.
* **launcher:** the five scalar REGISTRY_PROXY_* knobs are retired. Setting any of them now fails the launch gate. Declare registries in a REGISTRY_PROXY_ROUTES_FILE routes file instead; see MIGRATING.md.

### Performance Improvements

* **butler:** share one ledger snapshot per pass ([3de01c9](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/3de01c9c1ce1d0a897a1f9bfb0980ea514b63156)), closes [#3918](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/3918)
* **checks:** leave the bats shards out of checks-inbox ([78c6bc9](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/78c6bc9ff23b671ed6d2c5273f9c4969c9b1df4a))
* **daemon:** coalesce the tip fetch, memoise the self-path ([9ebbc11](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/9ebbc1192018e9676c26a3b6d924bdb517f8f98d))
* **forgejo:** cache the org-labels 404 per client ([f5269ea](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/f5269ea4eaa2d3129a05335d0b8524b265a20f62)), closes [#4034](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/4034)
* **image:** let the Box build two derivations at once ([65d07ee](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/65d07eee1ef0426debcf9f1cb661300d8addb1cf))
* **ledger:** add Snapshot for one-fetch batch reads ([7b9053a](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/7b9053a608d8a704f26cb54eb0bffb7e253c2798)), closes [#3918](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/3918)
* **orchestrator:** append each pass's seeded block, don't prepend it ([fa852f9](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/fa852f9e23c0e3bfaebae84db5e2d70b696a9015))
* **prompts:** two-tier check discipline ([655191f](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/655191fe58ade986a44f561704f245145a3b1010)), closes [#4408](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/4408)
* **registry-proxy:** serve the transport probe from cache ([3183b21](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/3183b214055f3c2995ad8527d6c663e5702ecdc4)), closes [#3113](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/3113)
* **seamtest:** link each seam binary once per run ([511b1a1](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/511b1a1247f12c795607fa4586d03b23fa1c2000)), closes [#4486](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/4486)


### Security

* **box:** install guards before recovery ([29f5998](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/29f599823c0151b31d61cdf27dd883b44eb8ff80)), closes [#4446](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/4446)
* **bwrap:** keep FORGEJO_TOKEN off the runner argv ([518cd71](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/518cd71f5be0116dc2be4efe40c923c40d4efe5d)), closes [#2861](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/2861)

* **daemon:** redact URL userinfo from child stderr ([9c0166a](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/9c0166a4d770d1a5595526b561ad41d26bfed4ae)), closes [#3566](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/3566)

* **forge:** contain local issue ids to the issues dir ([69d1760](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/commit/69d176091e73cd4232935f13a38c41d3adfeb0a2)), closes [#3075](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift-ab/issues/3075)
